/* PASSO 02
- Cole esse código no rodapé da página.

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  const observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
    entries.forEach(function (entry, i) {
      if (entry.isIntersecting) {
        entry.target.classList.add("visible");
        observer.unobserve(entry.target);
      }
    });
  }, {
    threshold: 0.15  /* elemento precisa estar 15% visível para disparar */
  });

  document.querySelectorAll(".fade-up").forEach(function (el) {
    observer.observe(el);
  });
});
</script>				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-6844f15 elementor-widget elementor-widget-html" data-id="6844f15" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="html.default">
					<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
    // só age em links âncora dentro do popup aberto
    const anchor = event.target.closest('.elementor-popup-modal[aria-modal="true"] a[href*="#"]');
    if (!anchor) return;

    const href = anchor.getAttribute('href');
    if (!href || href === '#') return;

    const targetId = href.substring(href.indexOf('#') + 1);
    const targetEl = document.getElementById(targetId);
    if (!targetEl) return;

    event.preventDefault(); // impede o comportamento padrão que causa o "pulo"

    // fecha o popup
    if (window.elementorProFrontend && elementorProFrontend.modules.popup) {
      elementorProFrontend.modules.popup.closePopup({}, event);
    }

    // espera o popup fechar e aí sim rola suavemente até a seção
    setTimeout(function () {
      targetEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }, 400);
  });
});
</script>				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-f92daf6 elementor-widget elementor-widget-html" data-id="f92daf6" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="html.default">
					<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  let lastScroll = 0;
  const header = document.querySelector('#menu-sticky');
  if (!header) return;

  window.addEventListener('scroll', function () {
    const currentScroll = window.pageYOffset;

    if (currentScroll > lastScroll && currentScroll > 100) {
      // rolando pra baixo → esconde
      header.classList.add('header-hide');
    } else {
      // rolando pra cima → mostra
      header.classList.remove('header-hide');
    }

    lastScroll = currentScroll <= 0 ? 0 : currentScroll;
  });
});
</script>				</div>
